# Phase-7 industry-specific repository joins — P7-INDUSTRY-JOINS

## Scope and evidence boundary

This lane is a bounded industry-specific join tranche from the Phase-3 deterministic GitHub candidate metadata, Phase-7 closure queue, the five-bucket Luna plan, Phase-2 industry atom specifications, and the Phase-5 standards applicability boundary. It does not claim complete 100-repository industry coverage, validated demand, capability, rights clearance, implementation, or admission.

- Industry universe: 17; discovery buckets: 85 (5 per industry); working bucket quota: 20; no underfilled bucket was padded.
- Join records: 745 total; selected defensible tranche: 55; held/unknown or recorded non-selected: 690.
- Underfilled buckets: 85/85; generic cross-vertical matches held: 563.
- Query/date/source provenance is copied from the Phase-3 register. Rate-limit events were absent from that input and remain `unknown_not_recorded`; this lane performed no URL fetch or new search.
- Every join carries atom/workflow/source-of-truth/state/terminal owner/exception/falsifier, canonical repository identity, rights/SBOM/maintenance limits, and explicit research-only boundaries.

## Selection rule and preserved hold states

- `selected` requires an exact recorded query or bounded niche text match to one of the five plan buckets and Phase-3 disposition `candidate`. The industry relation remains inference (`I`), not authenticated capability or demand.
- `hold`/`generic_hold_unknown` records preserve generic CRUD, platform-adjacent, cross-vertical, thin, or rights-uncleared signals without counting them toward a bucket quota.
- `reference`, `reject`, and other recorded dispositions remain non-selected and are not silently promoted.
- Existing closure-queue matches are summarized but never treated as complete unless their recorded status says complete; missing dimensions remain missing.

## Source, rights, and safety ledger

- Source identity is public repository/API/README/tree/path metadata already recorded in Phase 3. This lane did not fetch URLs, inspect source, clone, copy code, or issue new discovery requests.
- All 745 join records remain reference-only research relationships, including the 55 selected defensible joins; selection is not reuse approval, capability proof, implementation, or admission.
- `rate_limit_status` is explicitly unknown/not recorded because the Phase-3 candidate schema has no rate-limit event field; no rate-limit claim is made.
- License/API signals remain declared or unknown, rights are not cleared, provenance is not established, and generic descriptions/capability tags never satisfy a niche join alone.
- Maintenance is activity metadata only. Dependency/SBOM state is preserved from the register and remains unknown/not scanned.
- Healthcare, legal, mortgage, recruiting, finance, and other consequential industries remain synthetic/read-only administrative wedges only. No clinical, legal, underwriting, hiring, payment, booking, issuance, or external side effect is claimed.

## Post-write smoke contract

- Exact 17-industry and 85-bucket accounting; no bucket is silently padded.
- JSONL artifact IDs and industry×canonical-repository identities are unique; selected records are bucket-specific and disposition-candidate only.
- Every join has canonical identity, source query/date/URLs/class, atom/workflow/source-of-truth/state/owner/exceptions/falsifier, rights/SBOM/maintenance, and boundary flags.
- Generic CRUD/cross-vertical matches are present only as hold/unknown or recorded non-selected states.
- Markdown counts, local links, source URL syntax, JSONL shape, boundary flags, and whitespace-safe git checks are required.
